\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-5/4}\epsilon_{1}\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-2}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-1/2}\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta}\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}\times H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c(t-s)}\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta}\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}\times H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/4}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/4+(3+|a|)\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/4+(3+|a|)\delta}.

Step 3. Proof of the bound (
3.40
).
By the definition of
\tilde{F}^{(a)}_{2,III}
, we only prove the following term in detail

(3.43)

\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla e^{(t-s)B}(0,\nabla({\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}))^{\top}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-2}}+\lVert\nabla e^{(t-s)\Delta}\nabla({\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-2}}ds,

for
|b|+|c|\leq|a|
. Using (
2.6
) and (
2.4
), it follows that

\displaystyle(\ref{drra,-1/2,F2,III})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-7/4}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a)-2,1}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-1}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c(t-s)}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds.

By (
3.8
), (
3.9
) and (
2.21
), one may obtain

(3.44)

\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a)-2,1}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{3\delta}

and from (
2.21
), (
3.28
) and (
3.2
) we have

\displaystyle\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+4\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|c|)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}t^{-3/4+(2+|b|)\delta}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}.

Hence, by the above two bounds, we may bound (
3.43
) by

\displaystyle(\ref{drra,-1/2,F2,III})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}((t-s)^{-7/4}+e^{-c(t-s)})(\epsilon_{1}\sum_{|c|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{3\delta})ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t/2}^{t}(\langle t-s\rangle^{-1}+e^{-c(t-s)})(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}t^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta}\sum_{|c|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/4+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/4+(3+|a|)\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/4+(3+|a|)\delta}.

This implies the bound (
3.40
).

Finally, the bound (
3.41
) can also be obtained using the similar argument to that of the bounds (
3.39
) and (
3.40
). This completes the proof of (
3.29
).

Proof of (
3.30
) for
t>1
.

It suffices to prove

(3.45)

\displaystyle\lVert\nabla^{2}e^{tB}({\varrho}^{(a)}_{0},v^{(a)}_{0})^{\top}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}+\lVert\nabla^{2}e^{t\Delta}w^{(a)}_{0}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}t^{-1},

(3.46)

\displaystyle\sum_{|b|\leq|a|-1}(\lVert\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)B}(0,\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}ds\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}+\lVert\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)\Delta}\Delta u^{(b)}ds\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}})\lesssim\epsilon_{1}t^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta},

(3.47)

\displaystyle\lVert\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)B}(F_{1}^{(a)},\tilde{F}_{2,II}^{(a)})^{\top}ds\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}+\lVert\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)\Delta}{\mathbb{P}}\tilde{F}_{2,II}^{(a)}ds\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4+(4+|a|)\delta}

(3.48)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)B}(0,\tilde{F}_{2,III}^{(a)})^{\top}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}+\lVert\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)\Delta}\tilde{F}_{2,III}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta},

and

(3.49)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla e^{(t-s)B}(0,Z^{a}Err2)^{\top}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-2}}+\lVert\nabla e^{(t-s)\Delta}Z^{a}Err2\rVert_{H^{N(a)-2}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}.

then, by Duhamel’s formula and (
3.45
)-(
3.49
), the bound (
3.30
) follows.

Now we begin to prove the bounds (
3.45
)-(
3.49
), respectively. The bound (
3.45
) follows from (
2.6
), (
2.4
) and (
1.11
) directly.

Step 1. Proof of the bound (
3.46
).
By (
2.6
), (
2.4
), (
2.21
) and inductive assumptions, we have for any
|b|\leq|a|-1

\displaystyle\lVert\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)B}(0,\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}ds\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}+\lVert\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)\Delta}\Delta u^{(b)}ds\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-2}\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}ds+\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-1}\lVert\nabla^{2}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c(t-s)}\lVert\nabla^{4}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}t^{-1}+\epsilon_{1}t^{\delta-1+(2+|b|)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}t^{-2}\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{2}e^{-c(t-s)}ds+\epsilon_{1}t^{-1+(2+|b|)\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}t^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}.

Step 2. Proof of the bound (
3.47
).
By (
2.6
), (
2.4
) we have

(3.50)

\displaystyle\lVert\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)B}(F_{1}^{(a)},\tilde{F}_{2,II}^{(a)})^{\top}ds\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}+\lVert\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)\Delta}{\mathbb{P}}\tilde{F}_{2,II}^{(a)}ds\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-7/4}\lVert(F_{1}^{(a)},F_{2,II}^{(a)})\rVert_{W^{N(a)-5,1}}ds+\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-1}\lVert(F_{1}^{(a)},F_{2,II}^{(a)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c(t-s)}\lVert(F_{1}^{(a)},F_{2,II}^{(a)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}ds.

Then from Lemma
3.7
, (
3.28
), (
3.29
) and (
2.21
), we may bound this by

\displaystyle{\rm LHS}(\ref{d2rra,-1,Nonl})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-7/4}\epsilon_{1}\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-1}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-5/4+(4+|a|)\delta}ds+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c(t-s)}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-5/4+(4+|a|)\delta}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4+(5+|a|)\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4+(5+|a|)\delta}.

Step 3. Proof of the bound (
3.48
).
We only estimate the following term in detail

(3.51)

\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)B}(0,\nabla({\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}))^{\top}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}+\lVert\nabla^{2}e^{(t-s)\Delta}\nabla({\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}ds,

for
|b|+|c|\leq|a|
. In fact, it follows from (
2.6
) and (
2.4
) that

\displaystyle(\ref{d2rra,-1,F2,III})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-9/4}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a)-5,1}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-3/2}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-2}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c(t-s)}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-2}}ds.

Then by (
3.28
), (
3.29
) and (
3.7
), we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}+\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{W^{N(a)/2,\infty}}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-2}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a)/2,\infty}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-5/4+(4+|a|)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}t^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta},

which, combined with (
3.44
), yields

\displaystyle(\ref{d2rra,-1,F2,III})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}((t-s)^{-9/4}+e^{-c(t-s)})(\epsilon_{1}\sum_{|c|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{3\delta})ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t/2}^{t}(\langle t-s\rangle^{-3/2}+e^{-c(t-s)})\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}.

This implies the bound (
3.40
).

Finally, the bound (
3.49
) can also be obtained using the similar argument to that of (
3.47
) and (
3.48
). This completes the proof of (
3.30
).

∎

3.3.
Decay estimates of
\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)},\nabla u^{(a)}
for any
|a|\leq N_{1}

Due to the presence of dissipation, by the linear estimate (
2.7
), we have the following improved pointwise decay estimates.

Lemma 3.9
.

With the hypothesis in Proposition
2.8
, let
l(|a|+1)=l(|a|)/40
for
0\leq|a|\leq N_{1}
and
l(0)=10^{-2}
. For any
t\in[0,T]
we have

(3.52)

\lVert|\nabla|({\varrho},v,w)\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(0)+2\delta},

(3.53)

\lVert|\nabla|({\varrho}^{(a)},v^{(a)},w^{(a)})\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(|a|)/3+(2+|a|)\delta},\ {\rm for}\ 1\leq|a|\leq N_{1}-2,

(3.54)

\lVert|\nabla|({\varrho}^{(a)},v^{(a)},w^{(a)})\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta},\ {\rm for}\ N_{1}-1\leq|a|\leq N_{1}.

Proof.

Proof of the bound (
3.52
).

By Duhamel’s formula it suffices to show the bounds for
t>1

(3.55)

\displaystyle\lVert e^{tB}|\nabla|({\varrho}_{0},v_{0})\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}+\lVert e^{t\Delta}|\nabla|w_{0}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}t^{-5/4},

(3.56)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}(|\nabla|F_{1},\nabla\cdot F_{2})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1-l(0)+2\delta},

(3.57)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert e^{(t-s)\Delta}|\nabla|{\mathbb{P}}F_{2}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4}.

The first bound (
3.55
) is obtained by (
2.5
) and (
2.4
). Now we prove (
3.56
) and (
3.57
).

Step 1. We prove the bound (
3.56
).
By (
2.5
) we have

(3.58)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}(|\nabla|F_{1},\nabla\cdot F_{2})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-2}\lVert(F_{1},F_{2,I})\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,1}\cap H^{N(2)+1}}+(t-s)^{-5/2}\lVert\tfrac{1+\cos^{2}\phi_{2}}{1+{\varrho}}|\nabla\phi|^{2}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,1}\cap H^{N(2)+1}},

which, by Lemma
3.4
and (
2.21
) , can be bounded by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-2}\lVert(\nabla u,\nabla{\varrho})\rVert_{H^{N(2)+2}}(\lVert(u,{\varrho})\rVert_{H^{N(2)+1}}+\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(2)+1}}^{2})+(t-s)^{-5/2}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(2)+2}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}\epsilon_{1}t^{-2}\lVert(\nabla u,\nabla{\varrho})\rVert_{H^{N(2)+2}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/2}.

Next, let
k(0)
satisfy
2^{k(0)}\simeq\langle t\rangle^{-l(0)}
, by frequency decomposition
\mathbf{1}=P_{\leq k(0)}+P_{>k(0)}
, we may rewrite the integral

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}e^{(t-s)B}(|\nabla|F_{1},\nabla\cdot F_{2})^{\top}ds=

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}P_{\leq k(0)}e^{(t-s)B}(|\nabla|F_{1},\nabla\cdot F_{2})^{\top}ds+\int_{t/2}^{t}P_{>k(0)}e^{(t-s)B}(|\nabla|F_{1},\nabla\cdot F_{2})^{\top}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}_{\leq k(0)}+\mathbf{I}_{>k(0)}.

We estimate now the low frequency part and high frequency part, respectively.

Low frequency part:
\mathbf{I}_{\leq k(0)}
.
From (
2.5
), (
3.15
), (
3.16
) and (
3.2
), we get

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{I}_{\leq k(0)}\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-5/4}\big(\lVert(u,{\varrho})\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert(\nabla u,\nabla{\varrho})\rVert_{H^{2}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+2^{k(0)}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}t^{-3/2+2\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1-l(0)+2\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1-l(0)+2\delta}.

High frequency part:
\mathbf{I}_{>k(0)}
.
By (
2.7
), (
3.15
) and (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{I}_{>k(0)}\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-3/2}2^{-13k(0)}\lVert(|\nabla|F_{1},\nabla\cdot F_{2})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-13k(0)}\cdot\sup_{s\in[t/2,t]}\lVert(|\nabla|F_{1},\nabla\cdot F_{2})^{\top}(s)\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{13l(0)}\cdot\sup_{s\in[t/2,t]}\big(\lVert(u,{\varrho})(s)\rVert_{W^{N(2)+2,\infty}}^{2}+\sum_{k}2^{N(2)k}\lVert P_{k}\nabla\phi(s)\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi(s)\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{13/100}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/2+2\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-2+4\delta})\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4},

where we have used (
3.15
) and (
3.2
). Hence, the bound (
3.56
) follows.

Step 2. We prove the bound (
3.57
).

By (
2.4
) we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}\lVert e^{(t-s)\Delta}|\nabla|{\mathbb{P}}F_{2}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-2}\lVert F_{2,I}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,1}}+(t-s)^{-5/2}\lVert\frac{1+\cos^{2}\phi_{2}}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla\phi\nabla\phi\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,1}}ds,

which, by (
3.12
), (
3.8
) and (
2.21
), is further bounded by

\int_{0}^{t/2}\epsilon_{1}t^{-2}(\lVert\nabla{\varrho}(s)\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}+\lVert\nabla u(s)\rVert_{H^{N(0)}})+\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/2}.

Next, using (
2.4
), we get

(3.59)

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\lVert e^{t\Delta}|\nabla|{\mathbb{P}}F_{2}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t-1}(t-s)^{-5/4}\lVert F_{2,I}\rVert_{H^{N(2)-2}}+(t-s)^{-1}\lVert\frac{1+\cos^{2}\phi_{2}}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla\phi\nabla\phi\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t-1}^{t}\lVert|\nabla|F_{2}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}ds.

From (
3.15
), (
3.16
) and (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\lVert F_{2,I}\rVert_{H^{N(2)-2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert({\varrho},u)\rVert_{W^{N(2)-1,\infty}}\lVert\nabla({\varrho},u)\rVert_{H^{N(2)-1}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(2)-2}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-5/4+\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{-7/4+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{-5/2+4\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-5/4+\delta},

\displaystyle\lVert\frac{1+\cos^{2}\phi_{2}}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla\phi\nabla\phi\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim(1+\epsilon_{1})\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}^{2}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-2+4\delta}.

and

\lVert|\nabla|F_{2}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-5/4+\delta}.

Thus by these estimates, we get

{\rm LHS}(\ref{dw,-1-delta,t/2})\lesssim\int_{t/2}^{t-1}(t-s)^{-5/4}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-5/4+\delta}+(t-s)^{-1}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-2+2\delta}ds+\int_{t-1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-5/4+\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4+\delta},

which gives the bound (
3.57
). Thus this completes the proof of (
3.52
).

Proof of the bound (
3.53
).

We prove the bound (
3.53
) by induction. Precisely, by (
3.52
) we assume that the bound

(3.60)

\lVert|\nabla|({\varrho}^{(b)},v^{(b)},w^{(b)})\rVert_{W^{N(b+2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(|b|)/3+(2+|b|)\delta},\ {\rm for}\ |b|<|a|

holds, then we prove the bound (
3.53
).

Since the bound (
3.53
) for
w^{(a)}
can be proved by a similar argument to the bound (
3.53
) for
{\varrho}^{(a)}
and
v^{(a)}
using (
2.4
), (
2.21
), (
3.28
), (
3.29
), (
3.2
) and inductive assumptions (
3.60
), we only prove the bound (
3.53
for
{\varrho}^{(a)}
and
v^{(a)}
in detail here. By Duhamel’s formula it suffices to show the following bounds

(3.61)

\displaystyle\lVert e^{tB}|\nabla|({\varrho}_{0}^{(a)},v_{0}^{(a)})\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}t^{-5/4},

(3.62)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}(0,|\nabla|\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1-l(|a|)/3+(2+|b|)\delta},\ {\rm for}\ |b|<|a|,

(3.63)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}(|\nabla|\tilde{F}^{(a)}_{1},\nabla\cdot\tilde{F}^{(a)}_{2,II})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1-l(|a|)+4\delta},

(3.64)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}(0,\nabla\cdot\tilde{F}^{(a)}_{2,III}+\nabla\cdot(S+1)^{a_{1}}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}Err2)^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1-l(|a|)+4\delta}

The first bound (
3.61
) is obtained by (
2.5
). Next we show (
3.62
) and (
3.63
).

Step 1. Proof of the bound (
3.62
).
From (
2.5
) and (
2.21
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}(0,|\nabla|\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(2)-2,\infty}}ds\lesssim\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-3/4-3/2}\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(2)+3}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}t^{-5/4}.

Next, by frequency decomposition we divide the following integral into low frequency part and high frequency part, i.e

\int_{t/2}^{t}e^{(t-s)B}(0,|\nabla|\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}ds=\int_{t/2}^{t}P_{\leq k(|a|)}e^{(t-s)B}(0,|\nabla|\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}ds+\int_{t/2}^{t}P_{>k(|a|)}e^{(t-s)B}(0,|\nabla|\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}ds,

where
k(|a|)
satisfies
2^{k(|a|)}\simeq\langle t\rangle^{-l(|a|)}
. Then we estimate these two contributions separately.

Low frequency part.
By (
2.5
) and (
3.30
),

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\lVert P_{\leq k(|a|)}e^{(t-s)B}(0,|\nabla|\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-3/4-1/3}2^{k(|a|)/3}\lVert\Delta u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}t^{-1+(2+|b|)\delta-l(|a|)/3}.

High frequency part.
By (
2.7
), (
3.60
) and
l(|b|+1)=\frac{l(|b|)}{40}
for
0\leq|b|\leq N_{1}

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\lVert P_{>k(|a|)}e^{(t-s)B}(0,|\nabla|\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-3/2}2^{-13k(|a|)}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2),\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}t^{13l(|a|)-1-l(|b|)/3+(2+|b|)\delta+\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}t^{-1-l(|a|)/3+(2+|a|)\delta}.

This completes the proof of (
3.62
).

Step 2. Proof of the bound (
3.63
).
On one hand, by (
2.5
), Hölder and (
2.21
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}(|\nabla|F_{1}^{(a)},\nabla\cdot\tilde{F}_{2,II}^{(a)})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-2}\sum_{b+c=a}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{N(a+1)}}\Big(\lVert({\varrho}^{(c)},u^{(c)})\rVert_{H^{N(a+1)}}+\sum_{e+\tilde{e}=c}\lVert\Phi^{(e)}\rVert_{H^{N(a+1)}}\lVert\Phi^{(\tilde{e})}\rVert_{H^{N(a+1)}}\Big)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-3/2}(\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{2\delta})\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/2+2\delta}.

On the other hand, for the nonlinear terms
F_{1}^{(a)}
and
\tilde{F}_{2,II}^{(a)}
, by (
2.5
), (
3.28
) and (
3.29
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}(|\nabla|F_{1}^{(a)},\nabla\cdot\tilde{F}_{2,II}^{(a)})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-5/4}\lVert(F_{1}^{(a)},\tilde{F}_{2,II}^{(a)})\rVert_{H^{N(a+2)+1}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[t/2,t]}\Big[\sum_{b+c=a}\lVert({\varrho}^{(b)},u^{(b)})\rVert_{W^{2,\infty}}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)},\nabla u^{(c)})\rVert_{H^{N(a+2)+2}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\sum_{b+c+e=a}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a+2)+1}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi^{(e)}\rVert_{H^{N(a+1)}}\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4+(4+|a|)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}t^{-1/2+(2+|b|)\delta-1+4\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-5/4+(4+|a|)\delta}.

Step 3. Proof of the bound (
3.64
).
It suffices to estimate

\int_{0}^{t}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}\nabla^{2}(0,{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}\nabla^{2}(0,\Phi^{(b)}\Phi^{(c)})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds,

for
b+c=a
. The first term can be estimated by (
2.5
), (
2.21
), (
3.28
) and (
3.29
). For the second term, by (
2.5
) and (
2.21
), we have

\int_{0}^{t/2}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}\nabla^{2}(0,\Phi^{(b)}\Phi^{(c)})^{\top}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds\lesssim\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-5/2}\lVert\Phi^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a+1)}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a+1)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/2+2\delta}.

Next, by frequency decomposition, we divide the following integral into low frequency part and high frequency part,

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\lVert e^{(t-s)B}\nabla^{2}(0,\big(\Phi^{(c)}\Phi^{(e)}\big))\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\lVert P_{\leq k(|a|)}e^{(t-s)B}\nabla^{2}(0,\big(\Phi^{(c)}\Phi^{(e)}\big))\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t/2}^{t}\lVert P_{>k(|a|)}e^{(t-s)B}\nabla^{2}(0,\big(\Phi^{(c)}\Phi^{(e)}\big))\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle I_{\leq k(|a|)}+I_{>k(|a|)},

then we control these two parts separately. By (
2.5
) and (
2.21
), we have

\displaystyle I_{\leq k(|a|)}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-5/4}2^{k(|a|)}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\Phi^{(e)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k(|a|)}\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1+4\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1-l(|a|)+4\delta}.

By (
2.7
), (
2.21
) and (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle I_{>k(|a|)}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-3/2}2^{-13k_{0}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2),\infty}}\lVert\Phi^{(e)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2),\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-13k_{0}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-2+6\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/2}.

Thus the bound (
3.64
) follows and the proof of (
3.53
) completes.

Proof of the bound (
3.54
).

By Duhamel’s formula, (
2.4
) and (
2.5
) it suffices to show that

(3.65)

\displaystyle\lVert\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-s)B}\nabla(0,\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}+e^{(t-s)\Delta}|\nabla|{\mathbb{P}}\Delta u^{(b)}ds\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}t^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta},\ {\rm for}\ |b|<|a|,

(3.66)

\displaystyle\lVert\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-s)B}\nabla(F_{1}^{(a)},F_{2}^{(a)})^{\top}+e^{(t-s)\Delta}\nabla{\mathbb{P}}\tilde{F}_{2}^{(a)}ds\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}.

By (
2.5
), (
2.4
), (
2.21
) and (
3.30
), we have

\displaystyle\lVert\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-s)B}\nabla(0,\Delta u^{(b)})^{\top}+e^{(t-s)\Delta}|\nabla|{\mathbb{P}}\Delta u^{(b)}ds\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-9/4}\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a+2)+3}}ds+\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-5/4}\lVert\nabla^{2}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a+2)+1}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}t^{-5/4}+\epsilon_{1}t^{-1+(2+|b|)\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}t^{-1+(2+|b|)\delta}.

Next, we prove the bound (
3.66
). From the proof of (
3.53
), it suffices to prove that for
b+c=a

(3.67)

\displaystyle\lVert\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-s)B}\nabla(0,\nabla(\Phi^{(b)}\Phi^{(c)}))^{\top}+e^{(t-s)\Delta}\nabla^{2}(\Phi^{(b)}\Phi^{(c)})ds\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}.

We may assume that
|b|\leq|c|
, by (
2.5
), (
2.4
), (
2.21
) and (
3.2
) we may bound the left-hand side by

\displaystyle{\rm LHS}(\ref{d2phi^2})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}(t-s)^{-5/2}\lVert\Phi^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle t-s\rangle^{-7/4}\sum_{k}2^{(N(a+2)+2)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-3/2+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1+4\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}t^{-1+(2+|a|)\delta}.

This completes the proof of (
3.54
).
∎

An immediate consequence of the above lemma is the decay estimates for
\partial_{t}{\varrho}^{(a)},\partial_{t}u^{(a)}
and
\partial_{t}\Psi^{(a)}
.

Lemma 3.10
.

Under the assumptions (
2.21
) and (
2.22
), for any
t\in[0,T]
, we have

(i)

(3.68)

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lesssim\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}},\ {\rm for}\ |a|\leq N_{1},

(3.69)

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-3,\infty}}+\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-4,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(a)/4},\ \ {\rm for}\ |a|\leq N_{1}-2,

and

(3.70)

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{n}}\lesssim\sum_{|l|\leq|a|}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(l)},\nabla^{2}u^{(l)})\rVert_{H^{n}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+5\delta},\ \ \mathrm{for}\ n\leq N(a)-1,\ |a|\leq N_{1}.

(ii) For any
|a|\leq N_{1}-1
,

(3.71)

\lVert\partial_{t}\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta}.

In particular, if
|a|\leq N_{1}-2
, we have

(3.72)

\lVert\partial_{t}\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1+3\delta}\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}.

Proof.

(i)
The first bound (
3.68
) follows directly from
{\varrho}^{(a)}
-equation in (
2.15
). For the second bound (
3.69
), by
{\varrho}^{(a)}
-equation (
2.15
), (
3.52
) and (
3.53
) we have

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-3,\infty}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\nabla\cdot u^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-3,\infty}}+\sum_{b+c=a}\big(\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-3,\infty}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla\cdot u^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-3,\infty}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\nabla\cdot u^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,p}}+\sum_{|c|\leq|a|}\lVert(\nabla\cdot u^{(c)},\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)})\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-2,p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(a)/4}.

and similarly, by
u^{(a)}
-equation in (
2.15
) we have

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-4,\infty}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla^{2}u^{(b)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-4,\infty}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-4,\infty}}+\sum_{b+c=a}\big[\lVert u^{(b)}\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-4,\infty}}

\displaystyle+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-4,\infty}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla^{2}u^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-4,\infty}}+\lVert\nabla(\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)})\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-4,\infty}}\big]

Then by
|a|\leq N_{1}-2
, (
3.53
) and (
3.2
), we may bound this by

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a+2)-4,\infty}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(a)/4}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|b|)\delta-1}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-2+6\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(a)/4}.

This concludes the proof of (
3.69
).

Next, we prove the bound (
3.70
). In fact, from
u^{(a)}
-equation in (
2.15
) we have that

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{n}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{n+1}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{n}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|b|+|c|\leq|a|}\big[\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{n}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{n}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla^{2}u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{n}}\big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{b+c+e=a}\lVert Z^{b}(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}})\nabla(\nabla\phi^{(c)}\nabla\phi^{(e)})\rVert_{H^{n}}+\sum_{|c|\leq|a|}\lVert Z^{c}Err2\rVert_{H^{n}}.

Moreover, from (
2.21
) it follows that

\displaystyle\lVert(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla u^{(c)})\rVert_{H^{n}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{n}}+\lVert({\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla^{2}u^{(c)})\rVert_{H^{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{n}}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)},\nabla u^{(c)})\rVert_{H^{n}\times H^{n+1}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)},\nabla u^{(c)})\rVert_{H^{n}\times H^{n+1}},

and from (
3.7
) and (
2.21
) we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert Z^{b}(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}})\nabla(\nabla\phi^{(c)}\nabla\phi^{(e)})\rVert_{H^{n}}\lesssim(1+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{n}})\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\nabla\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{H^{n+1}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+4\delta}.

The term
\lVert Z^{a}Err2\rVert_{H^{n}}
is estimated using similar argument. Hence, the bound (
3.70
) follows.

(ii)
We prove the bound (
3.71
) first. In view of
\phi^{(a)}
-equation in (
2.15
), it suffices to prove that

(3.73)

\displaystyle\sum_{b+c=a}\big(\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}+\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}+\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{b+c+e=a}\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla(u^{(c)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(e)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}+\lVert Z^{a}Err2\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/4+(|a|+1)\delta}.

Here we only estimate the first term
\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}
in detail, the other terms in (
3.73
) are similar.

Case 1:
|a|\geq N_{1}-1
.

When
|b|\leq|c|
, using (
3.69
) and (
2.21
), it follows that

(3.74)

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\big[\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{(N(a)-3)k^{+}}\lVert\partial_{t}P_{k}u^{(b)}\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{\infty}\big]\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{H(c)\delta}\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{W^{N(|b|+2)-4,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{2\delta}\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{W^{N(|b|+2)-2,\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1}.

If
|b|>|c|
, it follows from (
3.70
) and (
3.2
) that

(3.75)

\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\lesssim\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\sum_{k}2^{(N(a)-3)k^{+}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{\infty}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+3\delta}.

Case 2:
|a|\leq N_{1}-2
.

Using (
3.69
), (
3.70
), (
3.2
) and (
2.21
), we get

(3.76)

\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\lesssim\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{\infty}+\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-3}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+5\delta}.

From (
3.74
), (
3.75
) and (
3.76
), the bound (
3.73
) for the first term is obtained.

Finally, we prove the bound (
3.72
). By
\phi^{(a)}
-equation in (
2.15
), it suffices to show

\displaystyle\sum_{b+c=a}\big(\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}+\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}+\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{b+c+e=a}\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla(u^{(c)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(e)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}+\lVert Z^{a}Err2\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1+3\delta}\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}.

Here we only estimate the term

\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}

in detail. By
|a|\leq N_{1}-2
, (
3.70
), (
3.2
) and (
3.69
), we may bound this by

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\sum_{|l|\leq|b|}\lVert\nabla u^{(l)}\rVert_{H^{n+1}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+5\delta})\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(b)/4+H(c)\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1}+\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1+3\delta}\sum_{|l|\leq|b|}\lVert\nabla u^{(l)}\rVert_{H^{n+1}}.

This completes the proof of the Lemma.

∎

4.
Energy estimates, I: Sobolev bounds

In this section we prove the energy bounds (
2.23
) for
a=0
.

4.1.
The bound on
{\varrho}
and
u

We start with the Sobolev bound in (
2.23
).

Proposition 4.1
.

With the notation and hypothesis in Proposition
2.8
, for any
t\in[0,T]
, we have

(4.1)

\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big(\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2}+\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\mathrm{div}u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\big)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

Step 1. Higher order estimate for
{\varrho}
and
u
.

We define the energy functional

\displaystyle E_{0}(t)=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{|n|\leq N(0)}\big[\frac{1}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}{\varrho}|^{2}+(1+{\varrho})|\partial^{n}u|^{2}dx+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}|\partial^{n}\nabla u|^{2}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})|\partial^{n}\mathrm{div}u|^{2}dxds\big].

From (
1.10
), we have

(4.2)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}E_{0}(t)=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})|\partial^{n}{\varrho}|^{2}-\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\partial^{n}{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}+{\varrho}\nabla\cdot u)dx

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\partial^{n}{\varrho}\nabla\cdot\partial^{n}u+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u\cdot\partial^{n}\big(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho}\big)dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}{\varrho}|\partial^{n}u|^{2}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u\cdot\partial^{n}\mathcal{N}dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u\cdot\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}})\partial^{n_{2}}\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta u+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}u\big)dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4},

where
\mathcal{N}
denoted as

\mathcal{N}:=-u\cdot\nabla u+\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}}\big[\frac{1}{2}\nabla(\cos^{2}\phi_{2}|\nabla\phi_{1}|^{2}+|\nabla\phi_{2}|^{2})-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{j}(\cos^{2}\phi_{2}\nabla\phi_{1}\partial_{j}\phi_{1}+\nabla\phi_{2}\partial_{j}\phi_{2})\big].

Then by using Hölder’s inequality, Sobolev embedding, (
1.10
) and the assumptions (
2.21
), we have the estimates of
R_{i}(i=1,\cdots,4)
in the following.

For the estimate of
R_{1}
, note that
P(1+{\varrho})=a(1+{\varrho})^{\gamma}
, by (
3.68
) we get

\displaystyle R_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert(1+{\varrho})^{\gamma-3}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big(\lVert\partial_{t}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert\partial_{t}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla^{N(0)}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}\big)+\lVert\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}\lVert u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|=N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\partial^{n}{\varrho}\cdot(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}{\varrho}+\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}{\varrho})dx

\displaystyle+\lVert\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert{\varrho}\nabla\cdot u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}})\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|n|=N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}u\cdot\nabla|\partial^{n}{\varrho}|^{2}dx,

By integration by parts, Sobolev embedding and (
2.21
), we may bound the second term by

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|n|=N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}u\cdot\nabla|\partial^{n}{\varrho}|^{2}dx=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|n|=N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(\nabla\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}u+\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla\cdot u)|\partial^{n}{\varrho}|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{2}}+1)\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2}\lesssim\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle R_{1}\lesssim(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}})^{2}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}},

which implies

(4.3)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}R_{1}(s)ds\lesssim(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}([0,T];H^{N(0)})}^{2}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}([0,T];H^{N(0)-1})}^{2})\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}([0,T];H^{N(0)})}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

For
R_{2}
, let
e\in\{(1,0,0),(0,1,0),(0,0,1)\}
, integration by parts gives

\displaystyle R_{2}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\partial^{n}{\varrho}\nabla\cdot\partial^{n}u+\partial^{n}u\cdot\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla\partial^{n}{\varrho}

\displaystyle+\partial^{n}u\cdot\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}{\varrho}+{\varrho}\partial^{n}u\cdot\partial^{n}\big(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho}\big)dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}-\nabla(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})\cdot\partial^{n}u\partial^{n}{\varrho}+\partial^{n}u\cdot\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}{\varrho}

\displaystyle-\partial^{e}({\varrho}\partial^{n}u)\cdot\partial^{n-e}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho})dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}.

Then from (
2.21
), we get

(4.4)

\int_{0}^{t}R_{2}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Finally, we estimate the terms
R_{3}
and
R_{4}
. By
{\varrho}
-equation in (
1.10
), Sobolev embedding and (
2.21
), one has

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}{\varrho}|\partial^{n}u|^{2}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla u)dx+R_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert u\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert\partial_{t}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert 1+{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}})^{2},

For the other terms in
R_{3}
, by integration by parts, Sobolev embedding and (
2.21
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u\cdot\partial^{n}(\mathcal{N}+u\cdot\nabla u)dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}-\frac{1}{2}\nabla\cdot u(\cos^{2}\phi_{2}|\nabla\phi_{1}|^{2}+|\nabla\phi_{2}|^{2})+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{j}u(\cos^{2}\phi_{2}\nabla\phi_{1}\partial_{j}\phi_{1}+\nabla\phi_{2}\partial_{j}\phi_{2})dx

\displaystyle-\sum_{1\leq|n|\leq N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{e}((1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u)\cdot\partial^{n-e}(\mathcal{N}+u\cdot\nabla u)dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}.

Hence, by Hölder, (
2.21
) and (
3.2
) we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}R_{3}(s)+R_{4}(s)ds\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}})^{2}+\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\nabla\phi(s)\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{3}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\langle s\rangle^{-1+2\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

This concludes the bound

(4.5)

E_{0}(t)\lesssim E_{0}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{s}E_{0}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Using (
2.21
), it is easy to obtain

(4.6)

\displaystyle\lVert\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}-a\gamma\rVert_{L^{\infty}}=a\gamma\lVert(1+{\varrho})^{\gamma-2}-1\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle a\gamma\lVert\int_{0}^{{\varrho}}\frac{d}{ds}(1+s)^{\gamma-2}ds\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\lVert(1+{\varrho})^{\gamma-3}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}.

Therefore, combining (
4.5
) and (
4.6
) we further obtain

(4.7)

\frac{a\gamma}{4}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\lVert\mathrm{div}u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds\lesssim E_{0}(t)\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Step 2. The estimate for dissipation of density.

Taking
\partial^{n}
on the
u
-equation of (
1.10
) and multiplying
\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}
, by integration by parts we have

(4.8)

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}\partial_{t}udx+\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho})dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}\big(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}}(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta u+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}u)+\mathcal{N}\big)dx.

We use integration by parts and
{\varrho}
-equation in (
1.10
) to rewrite the first term on the left hand side of (
4.8
) by

(4.9)

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}\partial_{t}udx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\big(\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}udx-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}udx\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\big(\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}udx+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla(\nabla\cdot u+u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}+{\varrho}\nabla\cdot u)\cdot\partial^{n}udx\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\big(\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}udx-\lVert\partial^{n}\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}+{\varrho}\nabla\cdot u)\partial^{n}\nabla\cdot udx\big).

For the second term on the left hand side of (
4.8
), one has

(4.10)

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho})dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\big(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}|^{2}dx+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})\partial^{n_{2}}\nabla{\varrho}dx\big).

Then from (
4.8
)-(
4.10
), we obtain

(4.11)

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}(\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}udx-\lVert\partial^{n}\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}|^{2}dx)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}+{\varrho}\nabla\cdot u)\partial^{n}\nabla\cdot udx-\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})\partial^{n_{2}}\nabla{\varrho}

\displaystyle+\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}\big(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}}(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta u+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}u)+\mathcal{N}\big)dx.

By Sobolev embedding, (
2.21
) and the following observation

\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}\Delta udx\leq\frac{1}{4}\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}|^{2}dx+C(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2},

we get

\displaystyle{\rm LHS}(\ref{LHS4.11})\leq

\displaystyle C\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+C\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}|^{2}dx

\displaystyle+C(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+C\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}).

Noticing that

\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}u=\frac{1}{2}(|\partial^{n}(u+\nabla{\varrho})|^{2}-|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}|^{2}-|\partial^{n}u|^{2}),

it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}(\lVert\partial^{n}(u+\nabla{\varrho})\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}-\lVert\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}-\lVert\partial^{n}u\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{|n|\leq N(0)-1}\frac{3}{4}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}|^{2}dx-C(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+C\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+C\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}).

Hence, by bootstrap assumption (
2.21
), Hölder and (
3.2
), we have

(4.12)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\lVert u+\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2}-\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2}-\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\sum_{l=1}^{N(0)}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\nabla^{l}{\varrho}|^{2}dxds-C(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Step 3. We prove the energy estimate (
4.1
)

In order to prove the bound (
4.1
), choosing

c_{1}:=\min\{\frac{1}{4},\frac{\mu_{1}}{4C(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\}

and multiplying (
4.12
) by
c_{1}
, then adding one to the inequality (
4.7
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}}{2}\lVert u+\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2}+\frac{a\gamma}{8}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\frac{3c_{1}}{4}\sum_{l=1}^{N(0)}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\nabla^{l}{\varrho}|^{2}dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\mu_{1}}{4}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\lVert\mathrm{div}u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2},

which implies the bound (
4.1
).

∎

4.2.
The bound on
\phi

Proposition 4.2
.

With the notation and hypothesis in Proposition
2.8
, for any
t\in[0,T]
, we have

\lVert\partial_{t}\phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

By (
1.10
), integration by parts, Sobolev embedding, (
3.68
), (
3.70
) and (
3.2
), it is easy to obtain the basic energy estimate

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})|\partial_{t}\phi|^{2}+|\nabla\phi|^{2}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}{\varrho}|\partial_{t}\phi|^{2}+(1+{\varrho})\partial_{t}\phi(-\partial_{t}u\cdot\nabla\phi-u\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\phi-u\cdot\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\phi)+Err)dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\partial_{t}\phi\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\partial_{t}\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert\partial_{t}\phi\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\partial_{t}u\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}((1+{\varrho})\nabla\cdot u+u\cdot\nabla{\varrho})|\partial_{t}\phi|^{2}dx

\displaystyle+\lVert\partial_{t}\phi\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}^{2}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{H^{1}}+\lVert\partial_{t}\phi\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho},\nabla u)\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}\langle t\rangle^{-1+2\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle t\rangle^{-2+7\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}(\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{2}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}})\langle t\rangle^{-1+2\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle t\rangle^{-5/4},

then from integration in time, we obtain

(4.13)

\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})|\partial_{t}\phi|^{2}+|\nabla\phi|^{2}dxds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Now it suffices to consider the following higher order energy

E_{1}(t):=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|n|=N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})|\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi|^{2}+|\partial^{n}\nabla\phi|^{2}dx.

From the system (
1.10
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}E_{1}(t)=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|=N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}{\varrho}|\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi|^{2}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}})\partial^{n_{2}}\Delta\phi dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{|n|=N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(-\partial_{t}u\cdot\nabla\phi-2u\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\phi-u\cdot\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\phi)+Err3)dx.

By (
3.69
) (
3.52
), (
3.2
) and (
2.21
), the first term can be estimated as

(4.14)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{|n|=N(0)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{s}{\varrho}|\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi|^{2}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}})\partial^{n_{2}}\Delta\phi dxds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}(\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{N(0)/2}|\nabla|{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}

\displaystyle+\lVert|\nabla|{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{N(0)/2}\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}})ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{-1+2\delta}\lVert|\nabla|{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}})ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Next we consider the second term.

Step 1. We prove the bound

(4.15)

I_{1}:=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(\partial_{s}u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3},\ \ {\rm for}\ |n|=N(0).

Integration by parts in time gives

\displaystyle I_{1}=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)dx\big|_{0}^{t}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}\ \partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi)dxds-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}^{2}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I_{11}-I_{12}-I_{13}-I_{14}.

By (
2.21
) and (
3.68
), the first two terms
I_{11}
and
I_{12}
can be estimated as

\displaystyle|I_{11}-I_{12}|\lesssim\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

For the third term
I_{13}
, from integration by parts and Hölder, we have

\displaystyle I_{13}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}(1+{\varrho})u\cdot\nabla|\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi|^{2}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n,n_{2}<n}(\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\partial_{s}\phi)dxds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}-\frac{1}{2}(u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}+(1+{\varrho})\nabla\cdot u)|\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi|^{2}dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}(\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{|n|/2}\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{|n|/2}\Phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}})ds,

then by (
3.52
), (
3.2
) and (
2.21
) we may bound this by

\displaystyle I_{13}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho},\nabla u)\rVert_{H^{2}}^{2}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds+\epsilon_{1}\int_{0}^{t}\big(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+3\delta}+\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\langle s\rangle^{-1+2\delta}\big)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{4}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

For the last term
I_{14}
, it follows from (
1.10
) that

\displaystyle I_{14}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\Big(\frac{\Delta\phi}{1+{\varrho}}\Big)\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi)\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}Err3\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I_{141}+I_{142}+I_{143}+I_{144}.

By integration by parts, we may estimate
I_{141}
by

\displaystyle I_{141}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}-\partial^{n}\partial_{i}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{i}\phi+\partial_{i}u\cdot\nabla\phi)+(1+{\varrho})\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}\Big(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}}\Big)\partial^{n_{2}}\Delta\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\nabla\cdot u|\partial^{n}\nabla\phi|^{2}-\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n,n_{2}<n}\partial^{n}\partial_{i}\phi\cdot(\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\partial_{i}\phi)-\partial^{n}\partial_{i}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(\partial_{i}u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds,

which, from (
2.21
), (
3.52
) and (
3.2
), can be estimated as

\displaystyle I_{141}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\partial^{n}\partial_{i}\phi\rVert_{2}(\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{|n|/2}\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{|n|/2}\Phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}})ds+\epsilon_{1}^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+2\delta}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}ds+\epsilon_{1}^{4}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Similarly, using (
2.21
), (
3.52
) and (
3.2
) we obtain

\displaystyle I_{142}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})|\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)|^{2}dx\Big|_{0}^{t}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}|\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)|^{2}dxds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{4}+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

and

\displaystyle I_{144}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}((1+{\varrho})\nabla\cdot u+u\cdot\nabla{\varrho})|\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)|^{2}dxds

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\sum_{n_{2}<n}\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\phi)dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{3}(1+\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2})(\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{|n|/2}\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{|n|/2}\Phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}})ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(\lVert u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(0)}})\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds+\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{-1+2\delta}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}})ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{5}.

Finally, let
e\in\{(1,0,0),(0,1,0),(0,0,1)\}
, by integration by parts, we rewrite
I_{143}
as

\displaystyle I_{143}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})[\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi)\cdot(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)+\sum_{|n_{2}|<|n|}\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi)\cdot(\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\phi)]dxds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big[\frac{1}{2}\partial_{s}((1+{\varrho})|u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi|^{2})-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{s}{\varrho}|u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1+{\varrho})(\partial_{s}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)\cdot(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)+(1+{\varrho})\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n,|n_{2}|<|n|}(\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\partial_{s}\phi)\cdot(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)

\displaystyle-(1+{\varrho})\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n,|n_{2}|<|n|}\partial^{n-e}(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi)\cdot\partial^{e}(\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\phi)

\displaystyle-\partial^{e}{\varrho}\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n,|n_{2}|<|n|}\partial^{n-e}(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi)\cdot(\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\phi)\Big]dxds,

By (
2.21
), (
3.68
) and (
3.70
), we have

\displaystyle I_{143}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\lVert u(s)\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert\Phi(s)\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}+\lVert\partial{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}})\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\big(\lVert\partial_{s}u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\big)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

Hence, the desired bound (
4.15
) follows.

Step 2. We prove the bound

(4.16)

I_{2}:=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi)dxds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3},\ \ {\rm for}\ |n|=N(0).

Since
I_{2}
can be rewritten as

I_{2}=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}-\frac{1}{2}\big(u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}+(1+{\varrho})\nabla\cdot u\big)(\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi)^{2}+(1+{\varrho})\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n,|n_{2}|<|n|}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot(\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\partial_{s}\phi)dxds,

it follow from (
3.52
) and (
3.2
) that

\displaystyle I_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla({\varrho},u)\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\partial^{n}\Phi\rVert_{L^{2}}(\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{|n|/2}\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\langle\nabla\rangle^{|n|/2}\Phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}})ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+3\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1+2\delta}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Step 3. We prove the bound

(4.17)

I_{3}:=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}(u\cdot\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\phi))dxds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4},\ \ {\rm for}\ |n|=N(0).

By integration by parts, we rewrite this by

\displaystyle I_{3}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot(u\cdot\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi))dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}=n,|n_{3}|<|n|}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot(\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla(\partial^{n_{2}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{3}}\phi))dxds

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle I_{31}+I_{32}.

The second term
I_{32}
can be estimated directly using Hölder and (
2.21
). By integration by parts in times, the first term
I_{31}
can be further rewritten as

\displaystyle I_{31}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi)\cdot(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)+\big(u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}+(1+{\varrho})\nabla\cdot u\big)\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)dxds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial_{s}\big(\frac{1}{2}(1+{\varrho})(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)^{2}\big)-\frac{1}{2}\partial_{s}{\varrho}|u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi|^{2}dx-(1+{\varrho})(\partial_{s}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)\cdot(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)

\displaystyle+\big(u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}+(1+{\varrho})\nabla\cdot u\big)\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi)dxds.

From (
3.68
), (
3.70
) and (
3.52
) we may bound this by

\displaystyle I_{31}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\lVert u(s)\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert\Phi(s)\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\lVert\partial_{s}u\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla({\varrho},u)\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{4}+\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\lVert\nabla({\varrho},u)\rVert_{H^{N(0)-1}}^{2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

Thus the bound (
4.17
) is obtained.

Step 4. We prove the bound

(4.18)

I_{4}:=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi\cdot\partial^{n}Err3(s)dxds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4},\ \ {\rm for}\ |n|=N(0).

In fact, this is a consequence of

\displaystyle\lVert\partial^{n}Err3(s)\rVert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\lVert\Phi\rVert_{H^{N(0)}}\lVert\Phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert|\nabla|^{-1}\Phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-5/4},

which is given by (
3.3
).

Hence, from (
4.14
)-(
4.18
) and the assumption (
1.11
), we have

E_{1}(t)=E_{1}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{s}E_{1}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Combining this and (
4.13
), we obtain

\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(0)\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})|\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi|^{2}+|\partial^{n}\nabla\phi|^{2}dx\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2},

which completes the proof of the Proposition.

∎

5.
Energy estimates, II: Weighted norms

In this section we prove the energy bounds (
2.23
) for
1\leq|a|\leq N_{1}
.

5.1.
The bound on
Z^{a}{\varrho}
and
Z^{a}u
for
1\leq|a|\leq N_{1}
.

We start with the weighted Sobolev bounds in (
2.23
).

Proposition 5.1
.

With the notation and hypothesis in Proposition
2.8
, for any
t\in[0,T]
, we have

(5.1)

\lVert{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big(\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}^{2}+\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\lVert\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}\big)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We prove the bound (
5.1
) by induction. From (
4.1
), we may assume that (
5.1
) holds for all
|a|\leq l-1\leq N_{1}-1
, it then suffices to prove (
5.1
) for
|a|=l
.

Step 1. Higher order estimates for
{\varrho}
and
u
.

We define the energy functional

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{0}(t)=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{|n|\leq N(a)}\big[\frac{1}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}{\varrho}^{(a)}|^{2}+(1+{\varrho})|\partial^{n}u^{(a)}|^{2}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}|\partial^{n}\nabla u^{(a)}|^{2}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})|\partial^{n}\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}|^{2}dxds\big].

By (
2.15
), we have

(5.2)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{0}(t)=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})|\partial^{n}{\varrho}^{(a)}|^{2}dx

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\partial^{n}{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}\sum_{b+c=a}C_{a}^{b}(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}+{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla\cdot u^{(c)})dx

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\partial^{n}{\varrho}^{(a)}\nabla\cdot\partial^{n}u^{(a)}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}\big(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\big)dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}{\varrho}|\partial^{n}u^{(a)}|^{2}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}F_{2}^{(a)}dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\sum_{1\leq l\leq a_{1}}C_{a_{1}}^{l}(-1)^{l}\partial^{n}\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta S^{a_{1}-l}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u

\displaystyle+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}S^{a_{1}-l}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u\big)+{\varrho}\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta u^{(a)}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}\big)dx

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{1}+\mathcal{R}_{2}+\mathcal{R}_{3}+\mathcal{R}_{4}+\mathcal{R}_{5},

We will estimate the five terms, respectively. First, we prove the bound

(5.3)

\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{1}(s)+\mathcal{R}_{2}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

In fact, by Hölder, we may bound left hand side of (
5.3
) by

\displaystyle{\rm LHS}(\ref{R1,R2})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big[\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert(1+{\varrho})^{\gamma-3}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}-\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\partial^{n}{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}{\varrho}^{(a)})dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{b+c=a}\lVert{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(c)-1}}\Big]ds,

which by (
2.21
), (
3.69
), and (
3.52
) is bounded by

\displaystyle{\rm LHS}(\ref{R1,R2})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)/4}+\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\frac{1}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(u\cdot\nabla\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}+\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla\cdot u)|\partial^{n}{\varrho}^{(a)}|^{2}dxds+\epsilon_{1}^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{3}+\int_{0}^{t}(\lVert u\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}})\lVert{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{3}+\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+3\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Next, we prove the bound

(5.4)

\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{3}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Integration by parts yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{3}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}-\partial^{n}{\varrho}^{(a)}\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\nabla\Big(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\Big)+\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n,|n_{1}|\geq 1}\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n_{1}}\Big(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\Big)\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}{\varrho}^{(a)}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\partial^{e}{\varrho}\ \partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n-e}\big(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\big)-{\varrho}\ \partial^{n+e}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n-e}\big(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\big)dx,

where
e\in\{(1,0,0),(0,1,0),(0,0,1)\}
.
By Hölder and (
2.21
), we may bound this by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{3}(s)ds\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}

\displaystyle+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{3}}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Nest we prove the bound

(5.5)

\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{4}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

By (
3.69
), it is easy to estimate the first term in the integrand of
\mathcal{R}_{4}
by

\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{s}{\varrho}|\partial^{n}u^{(a)}|^{2}dxds\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}ds\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)/4}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Then we consider the second term in the integrand of
\mathcal{R}_{4}
. From the definition of
F_{2}^{(a)}
(
2.17
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}F_{2}^{(a)}dx=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}F_{2,I}^{(a)}dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}F_{2,II}^{(a)}dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}(S+1)^{a_{1}}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}Err2dx

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{41}+\mathcal{R}_{42}+\mathcal{R}_{43},

where

\displaystyle F_{2,I}^{(a)}:=

\displaystyle-\sum_{b+c=a}C^{b}_{a}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla u^{(c)}-\sum_{b+c=a,|b|\geq 1}C^{b}_{a}Z^{b}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}

\displaystyle-\sum_{b+c=a}C^{b}_{a}Z^{b}\big(\frac{{\varrho}}{1+{\varrho}}\big)\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta(S-1)^{(c_{1})}\Gamma^{(c^{\prime})}u+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}(S-1)^{c_{1}}\Gamma^{c^{\prime}}u\big),

\displaystyle F_{2,II}^{(a)}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{b+c+e=a}C^{b,c}_{a}Z^{b}\Big(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}}\Big)\big[\frac{1}{2}\nabla\big(\nabla(S-1)^{c_{1}}\Gamma^{c^{\prime}}\phi\cdot\nabla(S-1)^{e_{1}}\Gamma^{e^{\prime}}\phi\big)

\displaystyle-\partial_{j}\big(\nabla(S-1)^{c_{1}}\Gamma^{c^{\prime}}\phi\cdot\partial_{j}(S-1)^{e_{1}}\Gamma^{e^{\prime}}\phi\big)\big].

On one hand, we consider the contribution of
\mathcal{R}_{41}
. By integration by parts, Hölder, and Sobolev embedding, we may bound
\mathcal{R}_{41}
by

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{41}=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})u^{(a)}F^{(a)}_{2,I}-\sum_{1\leq|n|\leq N(a)}\big(\partial^{e}{\varrho}\partial^{n}u^{(a)}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n+e}u^{(a)}\big)\partial^{n-e}F_{2,I}^{(a)}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big[\lVert(1+{\varrho})u^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}+\sum_{1\leq|n|\leq N(a)}\lVert\big(\partial^{e}{\varrho}\partial^{n}u^{(a)}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n+e}u^{(a)}\big)\rVert_{L^{2}}\Big]\lVert F^{(a)}_{2,I}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{3}})(\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{2}}+\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})\Big(\sum_{b+c=a}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{b+c=a}\sum_{|\tilde{b}|\leq|b|}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(\tilde{b})}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}(1+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(\tilde{b})}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}})^{|b|}(\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}+\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})\Big),

which is acceptable by (
2.21
).

On the other hand, we consider the contribution of
\mathcal{R}_{42}
. By the symmetry, we may assume that
|c|\leq|e|
. It suffices to consider the term

(5.6)

\sum_{|n|\leq N(a),|c+e|\leq|a|}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\partial^{n}\nabla(\nabla\phi^{(c)}\nabla\phi^{(e)})dx,

By integration by parts, we may estimate this by

\displaystyle(\ref{R42'})=\sum_{|n|\leq N(a),|c+e|\leq|a|}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}[\nabla{\varrho}\partial^{n}u^{(a)}+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\nabla u^{(a)}]\partial^{n}(\nabla\phi^{(c)}\nabla\phi^{(e)})dx,

we then use Hölder and Sobolev embedding to bound previous by

\displaystyle(\ref{R42'})\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a),|c+e|\leq|a|}(1+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{3}})\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\sum_{k}2^{N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1+4\delta}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}},

which is acceptable by (
2.21
). A similar argument could be made for
\mathcal{R}_{43}
.

Thus, this concludes the bound (
5.5
).

Finally, we prove the bound

(5.7)

\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{5}(s)ds\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{E}_{0}(t)+C\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Let
e\in\{(1,0,0),(0,1,0),(0,0,1)\}
, by integration by parts,
\mathcal{R}_{5}
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{5}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{1\leq l\leq a_{1}}C_{a_{1}}^{l}(-1)^{l}(1+{\varrho})\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\partial^{n}\nabla u^{(a)}\partial^{n}\nabla S^{a_{1}-l}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\partial^{n}\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}\partial^{n}\mathrm{div}S^{a_{1}-l}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u\big)dx

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{1\leq l\leq a_{1}}C_{a_{1}}^{l}(-1)^{l}\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}\nabla S^{a_{1}-l}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\mathrm{div}{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}\mathrm{div}S^{a_{1}-l}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u\big)dx

\displaystyle+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}{\varrho}u^{(a)}\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta u^{(a)}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}\big)dx

\displaystyle-\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(\partial^{e}{\varrho}\partial^{n}u^{(a)}+{\varrho}\partial^{n+e}u^{(a)})\cdot\partial^{n-e}\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta u^{(a)}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}\big)dx.

We then use Hölder, inductive assumption, and (
2.21
) to bound this by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{5}(s)ds\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\lVert\partial^{n}\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\lVert\partial^{n}\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}ds+C\sum_{|b|<|a|}\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{3}}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\lVert\partial^{n}\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\lVert\partial^{n}\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}ds+C\epsilon_{0}^{2}+C\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

This implies the bound (
5.7
) by definition of
\mathcal{E}_{0}
.

Hence, from (
5.3
)-(
5.7
) and (
1.11
), one obtains

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{0}(t)=\mathcal{E}_{0}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{d}{ds}\mathcal{E}_{0}(s)ds\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{E}_{0}(t)+C\epsilon_{0}^{2},

which, combining with (
4.6
), implies

(5.8)

\frac{a\gamma}{2}\lVert{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\lVert\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Step 2. The estimate for dissipation of density
{\varrho}^{(a)}
.

Taking
\partial^{n}
on the
Z^{a}u
-equation of (
2.15
) and multiplying
\partial^{n}\nabla Z^{a}{\varrho}
, from integration by parts we have

(5.9)

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}\partial_{t}u^{(a)}dx+\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)})dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta(S-1)^{a_{1}}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}(S-1)^{a_{1}}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u+F^{(a)}_{2}\big)dx.

By
{\varrho}
-equation in (
2.15
), the first term in the left-hand side of (
5.9
) can be rewritten as

(5.10)

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}\partial_{t}u^{(a)}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\big(\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}u^{(a)}dx-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}u^{(a)}dx\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\big(\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}u^{(a)}dx

\displaystyle-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}[\nabla\cdot u^{(a)}+\sum_{b+c=a}C_{a}^{b}(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}+{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla\cdot u^{(c)})]\partial^{n}\nabla\cdot u^{(a)}dx\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\big(\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}u^{(a)}dx-\lVert\partial^{n}\nabla\cdot u^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sum_{b+c=a}C_{a}^{b}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}+{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla\cdot u^{(c)})\partial^{n}\nabla\cdot u^{(a)}dx\big).

We use integration by parts to rewrite the second term in the left-hand side of (
5.9
) as

(5.11)

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)})dx

\displaystyle=
