\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\big(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}|^{2}dx+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})\partial^{n_{2}}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}dx\big).

Then from (
5.9
)-(
5.11
), Hölder, and (
4.6
), we obtain

(5.12)

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}(\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}u^{(a)}dx-\lVert\partial^{n}\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}|^{2}dx)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big[\sum_{b+c=a}C_{a}^{b}\partial^{n}(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}+{\varrho}^{(b)}\nabla\cdot u^{(c)})\partial^{n}\nabla\cdot u^{(a)}

\displaystyle-\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\sum_{|n_{1}|\geq 1,n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}(\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}})\partial^{n_{2}}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}

\displaystyle+\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n}\big(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}\Delta(S-1)^{a_{1}}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\nabla\mathrm{div}(S-1)^{a_{1}}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}u+F^{(a)}_{2}\big)\Big]dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{b+c=a}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+C\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}^{2}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}|^{2}dx+C(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert F_{2}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}.

Noticing that

\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}\cdot\partial^{n}u=\frac{1}{2}(|\partial^{n}(u+\nabla{\varrho})|^{2}-|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}|^{2}-|\partial^{n}u|^{2}),

by Sobolev embedding and (
3.7
), the nonlinear terms
F_{2}^{(a)}
can be bounded by

(5.13)

\displaystyle\lVert F_{2}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{b+c=a}(\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})(\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}})

\displaystyle+C\sum_{b+c=a}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{b+c=a}(\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})(\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}})+C\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+4\delta}.

Therefore, (
5.12
) can be further bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{|n|\leq N(a)-1}\big(\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}(\lVert\partial^{n}(u^{(a)}+\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)})\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}-\lVert\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2}-\lVert\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\partial^{n}\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}|^{2}dx\big)-C(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{b+c=a}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}^{2}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}(\sum_{b+c=a}(\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})(\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}})+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+4\delta}).

Then by integration in time and (
2.21
) we have

(5.14)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\lVert u^{(a)}+\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}^{2}-\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}^{2}-\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\sum_{l=1}^{N(a)}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\nabla^{l}{\varrho}^{(a)}|^{2}dxds-C(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Step 3. The energy estimate (
5.1
)

In order to prove the bound (
5.1
), choosing

c_{1}:=\min\{\frac{1}{4},\frac{a\gamma}{4},\frac{\mu_{1}}{4C(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\}

multiplying (
5.14
) by
c_{1}
and then adding one to the inequality (
5.8
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}}{2}\lVert u^{(a)}+\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}^{2}+\frac{a\gamma}{8}\lVert{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\frac{3c_{1}}{4}\sum_{l=1}^{N(a)}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{P^{\prime}(1+{\varrho})}{1+{\varrho}}|\nabla^{l}{\varrho}^{(a)}|^{2}dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\mu_{1}}{4}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+(\frac{\mu_{1}}{2}+\mu_{2})\lVert\mathrm{div}u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

This implies the bound (
5.1
). We complete the proof of the lemma.

∎

5.2.
The bound on
Z^{a}\phi
for
1\leq|a|\leq N_{1}
.

Proposition 5.2
.

With the notation and hypothesis in Proposition
2.8
, for any
t\in[0,T]
, we have

\lVert\partial_{t}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

Proof.

By
\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}
it suffices to show that

{\mathcal{E}}_{a}(t):=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})|\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi^{(a)}|^{2}|+|\partial^{n}\nabla\phi^{(a)}|^{2}dx\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

From
\phi^{(a)}
-equation in (
2.15
) and integration by parts we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}{\mathcal{E}}_{a}(t)=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}{\varrho}|\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi^{(a)}|^{2}dx+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|=N(a),|n_{1}|\geq 1,\\
n_{1}+n_{2}=n\end{subarray}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n_{1}}\big(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}}\big)\partial^{n_{2}}\Delta\phi^{(a)}dx

\displaystyle-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|\in\{0,N(a)\},\\
b+c=a,|b|\geq 1\end{subarray}}C_{a}^{b}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(Z^{b}(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}})\Delta\phi^{(c)}\big)dx

\displaystyle-2\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|\in\{0,N(a)\},\\
b+c=a\end{subarray}}C_{a}^{b}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\phi^{(c)}\big)dx

\displaystyle-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|\in\{0,N(a)\},\\
b+c=a\end{subarray}}C_{a}^{b}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\big)dx

\displaystyle-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|\in\{0,N(a)\},\\
b+c+e=a\end{subarray}}C_{a}^{b,c}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla(u^{(c)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(e)})\big)dx

\displaystyle+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{t}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}(S+2)^{a_{1}}\Gamma^{a^{\prime}}Err3dx

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{R}}_{1}+{\mathbf{R}}_{2}+{\mathbf{R}}_{3}+{\mathbf{R}}_{4}+{\mathbf{R}}_{5}+{\mathbf{R}}_{6}.

Step 1. We prove the bound

\int_{0}^{t}{\mathbf{R}}_{1}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

By (
3.69
) we may bound the first term in
{\mathbf{R}}_{1}
by

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{s}{\varrho}|\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}|^{2}dx\lesssim\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)/4+2H(a)\delta}.

For the second term in
{\mathbf{R}}_{1}
, when
|a|\geq N_{1}-2
, by (
3.52
) we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|=N(a),|n_{1}|\geq 1,\\
n_{1}+n_{2}=n\end{subarray}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n_{1}}\big(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}}\big)\partial^{n_{2}}\Delta\phi^{(a)}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{W^{N(a),\infty}}(1+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})^{|n_{1}|}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+2\delta+2H(a)\delta};

when
|a|\leq N_{1}-3
, by (
3.52
) and (
3.2
) we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|=N(a),|n_{1}|\geq 1,\\
n_{1}+n_{2}=n\end{subarray}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n_{1}}\big(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}}\big)\partial^{n_{2}}\Delta\phi^{(a)}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\big(\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{W^{N(a)/2,\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a)/2,\infty}}\big)(1+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})^{|n_{1}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+2\delta+2H(a)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1+\delta+H(a+2)\delta}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}.

Thus combining these bounds and (
2.21
), we get

\int_{0}^{t}{\mathbf{R}}_{1}(s)ds\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+3\delta+2H(a)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1+\delta+H(a+2)\delta}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Step 2. We prove the bound

\int_{0}^{t}{\mathbf{R}}_{2}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

Noticing that for any
|b|\geq 1

Z^{b}(\frac{1}{1+{\varrho}})=\sum_{b_{1}+\cdots+b_{s}=b}C_{b_{1}\cdots b_{s}}(1+{\varrho})^{-s-1}{\varrho}^{(b_{1})}\cdots{\varrho}^{(b_{s})}.

By Hölder and (
2.21
), it suffices to prove

(5.15)

\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big({\varrho}^{(b)}\Delta\phi^{(c)}\big)dxds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta},

for
|b|+|c|\leq|a|,|b|\geq 1
.

For the contribution when
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,|c|\leq 2
, by Hölder, (
2.21
) and (
3.2
) we may bound the left-hand side of (
5.15
) by

\displaystyle{\rm LHS}(\ref{bfR2-rep})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\sum_{k}2^{N(a)k^{+}+k}\lVert P_{k}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

We now consider the contribution when
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,|b|\geq 1,|c|\geq 3
or
|a|\leq N_{1}-2,|b|\geq 1
. We decompose the left hand side of (
5.15
) dyadically in frequency, and use (
2.20
) to rewrite the functions
\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}
and
|\nabla|\phi^{(c)}
in terms of the profiles
\Psi^{(a)}
and
\Psi^{(c)}
, thus it suffices to estimate

\mathfrak{R}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}:=\int_{1}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}e^{-is(|\xi|-|\eta|)}(1+\xi^{2n})|\eta|\overline{\widehat{P_{k}\Psi^{(a)}}}(\xi)\widehat{P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\xi d\eta ds.

Low-high interaction: the contribution when
k_{1}\leq k+6
,
k-5\leq k_{2}\leq k+5
.

By integration by parts in
\eta
, we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle i\int_{1}^{t}s^{-1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\nabla_{\eta}\big(|\eta|(1+\xi^{2n})\big)\overline{\widehat{P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}}}(\xi)\widehat{P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)

\displaystyle+|\eta|(1+\xi^{2n})\overline{\widehat{P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}}}(\xi)\nabla_{\eta}\widehat{P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)

\displaystyle+|\eta|(1+\xi^{2n})\overline{\widehat{P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}}}(\xi)\widehat{P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)e^{is|\eta|}\nabla_{\eta}\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\xi d\eta ds.

We then use (
2.9
) and Hölder to bound this by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}\leq k+6,k_{2}=k+O(1)}\mathfrak{R}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}s^{-1}\sum_{k_{1}\leq k+6,k_{2}=k+O(1)}\Big[2^{2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+2^{2N(a)k^{+}+k_{2}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\mathcal{F}^{-1}(\nabla_{\eta}\widehat{P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}(\eta)})\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle 2^{2N(a)k^{+}+k_{2}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla_{\eta}\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}(\eta)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\Big]ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}s^{-1}\lVert\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\Big[\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\sum_{k_{1}}\lVert\langle r\rangle P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)+1}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{F}}^{-1}(|\xi|\nabla_{\xi}\widehat{\Psi^{(c)}})\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\Big]ds.

Since from (
2.13
), (
2.21
), and
|b|\leq N_{1}-2
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}}\lVert\langle r\rangle P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\lVert\partial_{r}\tilde{\Omega}^{\alpha}P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}\lVert\tilde{\Omega}^{\alpha}P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}}2^{k_{1}^{-}/2-k_{1}^{+}/2}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\lVert\tilde{\Omega}^{\alpha}P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{2}}^{1/2}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}\lVert\tilde{\Omega}^{\alpha}P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}\lVert\tilde{\Omega}^{\alpha}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}.

Combining this estimate, (
2.21
) and (
2.22
) we may continue to bound
\mathfrak{R}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}\leq k-5,k_{2}=k+O(1)}\mathfrak{R}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}s^{-1}\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{H(c)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{H(c+1)\delta})ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1+2H(a)\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

This concludes the treatment of low-high interaction.

High-low interaction: The contribution when
k-3<k_{1}<k+3,k_{2}<k-5
.

Integration by parts in time, we divide
\mathfrak{R}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
into four terms

(5.16)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle i\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\frac{(1+\xi^{2n})|\eta|}{|\xi|-|\eta|}\overline{\widehat{P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}}}(\xi)\widehat{P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\xi d\eta\Big|_{1}^{t}

\displaystyle-i\int_{1}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\frac{(1+\xi^{2n})|\eta|}{|\xi|-|\eta|}\overline{e^{is|\xi|}\widehat{P_{k}\partial_{s}\Psi^{(a)}}}(\xi)\widehat{P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\xi d\eta ds

\displaystyle-i\int_{1}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\frac{(1+\xi^{2n})|\eta|}{|\xi|-|\eta|}\overline{\widehat{P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}}}(\xi)\widehat{P_{k_{1}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\xi d\eta ds

\displaystyle-i\int_{1}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\frac{(1+\xi^{2n})|\eta|}{|\xi|-|\eta|}\overline{\widehat{P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}}}(\xi)\widehat{P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)e^{is|\eta|}\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\partial_{s}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\xi d\eta ds

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\mathfrak{R}^{3}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}.

Case 1. The contribution of
\mathfrak{R}^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

By (
2.1
) and (
2.21
), we may control this by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}\mathfrak{R}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}2^{k_{2}-k+2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{H(a)\delta+H(c)\delta}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

Case 2: The contribution of
\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

By (
2.1
), (
3.71
) and (
2.21
), we may bound this contribution when
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,|b|\geq 1,|c|\geq 3
by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}2^{k_{2}-k+2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\partial_{s}\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{6}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{3}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-5}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)+4}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta+H(c)\delta}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(b)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

When
|a|\leq N_{1}-2
, we utilize (
3.72
), (
3.2
) and (
2.21
) to control the contribution by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}2^{k_{2}-k+2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\partial_{s}\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\sum_{k_{2}}2^{k_{2}^{+}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{-1+3\delta}\sum_{|\tilde{b}|\leq|a|}\lVert\nabla u^{(\tilde{b})}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Case 3: The contribution of
\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

By (
2.1
), (
2.21
) and (
3.69
) we may bound this contribution when
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,|b|\geq 1,|c|\geq 3
by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}2^{k_{2}-k+2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\lVert\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\sum_{k_{1}}2^{N(a)k_{1}^{+}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1-l(b)/4+H(c)\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

By (
2.21
) and (
3.68
) we may bound this contribution when
|a|\leq N_{1}-2,|b|\geq 1
by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}2^{k_{2}-k+2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\lVert\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta}\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Case 4: The contribution of
\mathfrak{R}^{3}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

By (
3.71
) and (
2.21
) we bound this contribution by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5}\mathfrak{R}^{3}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5,\\
k_{1}\geq 0\end{subarray}}2^{k_{2}-k+2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\partial_{s}\Psi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}=k+O(1),k_{2}\leq k-5,\\
k_{1}<0\end{subarray}}2^{k_{2}-k}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\partial_{s}\Psi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\lVert\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert\partial_{s}\Psi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-3/4+(2+|c|)\delta}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

This concludes the treatment of high-low interaction.

High-high interaction: the contribution when
k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1)
,
k_{2}>k+5
.

This case will follows as easily as the high-low interaction, by (
5.16
) it suffices to show

\sum_{k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1)}(\mathfrak{R}^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\mathfrak{R}^{3}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}})\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

Case 1: The contribution of
\mathfrak{R}^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

By (
2.21
) we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1)}\mathfrak{R}^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1)}2^{2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

Case 2: The contribution of
\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

By (
3.71
) and (
3.28
) we may bound this contribution by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1)}\mathfrak{R}^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\sum_{k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1)}2^{2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\partial_{s}\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2+\delta}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2(2+\delta)/\delta}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)/2}}\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{W^{N(a)/2,2(2+\delta)/\delta}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4+(2+|a|)\delta-3/4+(3+|b|)\delta+H(c)\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

Case 3: The contribution of
\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

By (
2.1
), (
2.21
) and (
3.69
) we may bound this contribution when
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,|b|\geq 1,|c|\geq 3
by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1)}\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1),\\
k<0\end{subarray}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2+\delta}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2(2+\delta)/\delta}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1),\\
k\geq 0\end{subarray}}2^{2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\lVert\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\sum_{k_{1}}2^{k_{1}^{+}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2(2+\delta)/\delta}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1-l(b)/5+H(c)\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

By (
2.21
), (
3.68
) and (
3.2
) we may bound this contribution when
|a|\leq N_{1}-2,|b|\geq 1
by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1)}\mathfrak{R}^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1),\\
k<0\end{subarray}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2+\delta}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2(2+\delta)/\delta}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1),\\
k\geq 0\end{subarray}}2^{2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\lVert\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\sum_{k_{2}}2^{2k_{2}^{+}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2(2+\delta)/\delta}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta}\sum_{|b|\leq|a|}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Case 4: The contribution of
\mathfrak{R}^{3}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

By (
3.71
) and (
2.21
) we bound this contribution by

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1)}\mathfrak{R}^{3}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1),\\
k\geq 0\end{subarray}}2^{2N(a)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\partial_{s}\Psi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2}>k+5,k_{1}=k_{2}+O(1),\\
k<0\end{subarray}}\lVert P_{k}\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2+\delta}}\lVert P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2(2+\delta)/\delta}}\lVert P_{k_{2}}\partial_{s}\Psi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\lVert\Phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert\partial_{s}\Psi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-3/4+(2+|c|)\delta}\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

This concludes the treatment of the high-high interaction, and thus of Step 2.

Step 3. We prove the bound

\int_{0}^{t}{\mathbf{R}}_{3}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

Case 1: Prove the bound

\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\},b+c=a}{\varrho}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\big)dxds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

When
c=a
, integration by parts in space yields

(5.17)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}{\varrho}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big)dxds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}{\varrho}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}+\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n}\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big)dxds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}\Big[-\frac{1}{2}(u\cdot\nabla{\varrho}+{\varrho}\nabla\cdot u)|\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n_{1}+n_{2}=n,\\
|n_{1}|\geq 1\end{subarray}}{\varrho}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big(\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big)\Big]dxds,

By (
3.15
) and (
2.21
) we may control this term by

\displaystyle{\rm LHS}(\ref{case3(a)-c=a})\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4+\delta+2H(a)\delta}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

When
|c|\leq|a|-1
, by Hölder we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|\in\{0,N(a)\},\\
b+c=a,|c|\leq|a|-1\end{subarray}}{\varrho}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\big)dx\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{\infty}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert u^{(b)}\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4+\delta+H(a)\delta}\lVert u^{(b)}\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds.

Noticing that by
|c|\leq|a|-1
and (
2.21
) we have

\lVert u^{(b)}\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lesssim\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)+1}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{H(c)\delta}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}.

Combining these estimates we see that

\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4+H(a)\delta}\lVert u^{(b)}\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-3/4+\delta+H(a)\delta+H(c)\delta}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

This concludes the bound of Case 1.

Case 2: Prove the following bound for
c=a
or
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,|c|\leq 2

\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|\in\{0,N(a)\},\\
b+c=a\end{subarray}}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\big)dx\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

When
c=a
, by integration by parts, (
3.52
) and (
3.2
) we may bound the left-hand side by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big)dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}\Big[-\frac{1}{2}\nabla\cdot u|\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}|^{2}+\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big(\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n,|n_{1}|\geq 1}\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{2}}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big)\Big]dxds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\sum_{|a|\geq N_{1}-1}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{W^{N(a)-1,\infty}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|a|\leq N_{1}-2}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a),\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(0)+3\delta+2H(a)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1+\delta+H(a+2)\delta}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

When
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,|c|\leq 2
, by (
3.2
) we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\big)dx\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a)+1,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

Case 3: Prove the following bound for
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,3\leq|c|\leq|a|-1
or
|a|\leq N_{1}-2,|c|\leq|a|-1
,

(5.18)

\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a)\}}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}\big)dx\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

We decompose dyadically in frequency and rewrite the functions
\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}
and
\partial_{s}\phi^{(c)}
in terms of the profiles
\Psi^{(a)}
and
\Psi^{(c)}
, thus it suffices to estimate

\mathfrak{Q}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}:=\int_{1}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}e^{-is(|\xi|-|\eta|)}(1+\xi^{2n})\eta\overline{\widehat{P_{k}\Psi^{(a)}}}(\xi)\widehat{P_{k_{1}}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\xi d\eta ds.

This bound has similarity with the analysis in
Step 2
in this Lemma. Hence, we may use the same argument and the corresponding properties of
u^{(b)}
to obtain the bound (
5.18
). This completes the treatment of
Step 3
.

Step 4. We prove the bound

\int_{0}^{t}{\mathbf{R}}_{4}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

We first consider the contribution when
|n|=0
. Notice that when
|b|\leq|c|
, by (
3.69
) and (
2.21
) we have

\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(b)/4+H(c)\delta},

when
|b|>|c|
, by (
3.70
) and (
3.2
) we have

\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim(\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{2}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1/2})\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta}.

From these estimates and (
2.21
) we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{b+c=a}C_{a}^{b}(1+{\varrho})\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big(\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\big)dxds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert 1+{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(1+\epsilon_{1})\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta}\big(\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)},\nabla u^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{2}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(b)/4+H(c)\delta}\big)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Next we prove the following bound

(5.19)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a),b+c=a}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\big)dxds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

.

We divide the left-hand side into three terms

\displaystyle{\rm LHS}(\ref{case4(b)})=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big(\partial^{n}\partial_{s}u^{(a)}\cdot\nabla\phi\big)dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big(\sum_{n_{1}+n_{2}=n,|n_{2}|\geq 1}\partial^{n_{1}}\partial_{s}u^{(a)}\cdot\partial^{n_{2}}\nabla\phi\big)dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a),b+c=a,|b|<|a|}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\big)dxds

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{1}+\mathfrak{I}_{2}+\mathfrak{I}_{3}

Case 1: The contribution of
\mathfrak{I}_{1}
.

By integration by parts in time,
\mathfrak{I}_{1}
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{1}=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big(\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\nabla\phi\big)dx\big|_{0}^{t}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}\partial_{s}{\varrho}\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big(\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\nabla\phi\big)dxds

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big(\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{s}\phi\big)dxds

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}^{2}\phi^{(a)}\big(\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\nabla\phi\big)dxds

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{11}+\mathfrak{I}_{12}+\mathfrak{I}_{13}+\mathfrak{I}_{14}.

By (
3.2
), (
3.68
) and (
2.21
) we can bound the first three terms
\mathfrak{I}_{11},\mathfrak{I}_{12}
and
\mathfrak{I}_{13}
by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{11}+\mathfrak{I}_{12}+\mathfrak{I}_{13}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla\partial_{s}\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1+2\delta}+\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1+2\delta}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

For
\mathfrak{I}_{14}
, let
e\in\{(1,0,0),(0,1,0),(0,0,1)\}
, from integration by parts in space and
\phi^{(a)}
-equation in (
2.15
) we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{14}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}\partial^{n-e}\partial_{s}^{2}\phi^{(a)}[\partial^{e}{\varrho}\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\nabla\phi+(1+{\varrho})\partial^{e}(\partial^{n}u^{(a)}\cdot\nabla\phi)]dxds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}^{2}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}(1+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}})\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{W^{1,\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta}(1+\epsilon_{1})\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{-1+2\delta}\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Case 2: The contribution of
\mathfrak{I}_{2}
.

When
|a|\geq N_{1}-1
, by (
2.21
), (
3.10
) and (
3.2
) we may bound this contribution by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert 1+{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{W^{N(a),\infty}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(1+\epsilon_{1})\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta}(\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1/2})\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{-1+2\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

When
|a|\leq N_{1}-2
, by (
2.21
), (
3.70
), (
3.2
) and (
3.69
) we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert 1+{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\big[\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{W^{N(a)/2,\infty}}+\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(a)}\rVert_{W^{N(a)/2,\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\big]ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(1+\epsilon_{1})\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta}\big[(\lVert\nabla u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert\nabla{\varrho}^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)-1}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1/2})\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{-1+2\delta}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(a)/4+H(a)\delta}\big]ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

Case 3: The contribution of
\mathfrak{I}_{3}
.

This case follows as easily as
Case 4(b).2
above. Before estimate
\mathfrak{I}_{3}
, we control the term
\partial^{n}(\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)})
for
|n|=N(a),|b|<|a|
. When
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,|b|\leq|c|
, we have

\displaystyle\lVert\partial^{n}(\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)})\rVert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\rVert_{W^{N(a),\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(b)/4+H(c)\delta};

when
|a|\geq N_{1}-1,|b|>|c|
, by (
3.10
) and (
3.2
) we have

\displaystyle\lVert\partial^{n}(\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)})\rVert_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a),\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\lVert(\nabla u^{(b)},\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1/2})\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta};

when
|a|\leq N_{1}-2
, we have

\displaystyle\lVert\partial^{n}(\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)})\rVert_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\lVert(\nabla u^{(b)},\nabla{\varrho}^{(b)})\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1/2})\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1-l(b)/4+H(c)\delta}.

Combining these estimates we may bound this contribution by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{3}\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\lVert 1+{\varrho}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\partial^{N(a)}(\partial_{s}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)})\rVert_{L^{2}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}.

This concludes the bound (
5.19
), and thus of
Step 4
.

Step 5. We prove the bound

\int_{0}^{t}{\mathbf{R}}_{5}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

Case 1: The contribution when
e=a,|n|=N(a)
.

We may rewrite this contribution by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u\cdot\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\phi^{(a)})\big)dxds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}u\cdot\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi^{(a)})dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n_{1}+n_{2}+n_{3}=n,\\
|n_{3}|<|n|\end{subarray}}\partial^{n_{1}}u\cdot\nabla(\partial^{n_{2}}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n_{3}}\phi^{(a)})dxds

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{1}+\mathfrak{L}_{2}.

By integration by parts, we can estimate the first term
\mathfrak{L}_{1}
by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{1}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}\big[-(1+{\varrho})u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}-(u\cdot{\varrho}+(1+{\varrho})\nabla\cdot u)\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big](u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi^{(a)})dxds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}-\frac{1}{2}(1+{\varrho})|u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi^{(a)}|^{2}dx\Big|_{0}^{t}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{s}{\varrho}|u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi^{(a)}|^{2}dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|=N(a)}\big[-(1+{\varrho})\partial_{s}u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi^{(a)}-(u\cdot{\varrho}+(1+{\varrho})\nabla\cdot u)\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\big](u\cdot\nabla\partial^{n}\phi^{(a)})dxds.

Applying (
2.21
), (
3.68
) and (
3.70
) we can bound this by

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\lVert u\rVert_{H^{2}}^{2}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}^{2}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}(\lVert\partial_{s}u\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert(\nabla{\varrho},\nabla u)\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}})\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}+\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}^{2}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}(\lVert(\nabla{\varrho},\nabla u)\rVert_{H^{4}}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle s\rangle^{-1/2})\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

For second term
\mathfrak{L}_{2}
, by Hölder and (
2.21
) we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{2}\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}^{2}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

Case 2: The contribution when
e=a,|n|=0
and
|e|<|a|
.

By Hölder and (
2.21
) we control this by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}(1+{\varrho})\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}u\cdot\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\phi^{(a)})

\displaystyle\ \ \ +\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|n|\in\{0,N(a)\},\\
b+c+e=a,|e|<|a|\end{subarray}}C_{a}^{b,c}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla(u^{(c)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(e)})\big)dxds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(1+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{2}})\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{L^{2}}\lVert\nabla u\rVert_{H^{2}}^{2}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{2}}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{b+c+e=a,|e|<|a|}(1+\lVert{\varrho}\rVert_{H^{2}})\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)+1}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

This completes the proof of
Step 5
.

Step 6. We prove the bound

\int_{0}^{t}{\mathbf{R}}_{6}(s)ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{2H(a)\delta}.

In order to deal with this contribution, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\sum_{|n|\in\{0,N(a),b+c+e=a\}}(1+{\varrho})\partial^{n}\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\partial^{n}\big(\phi^{(b)}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\nabla\phi^{(e)}\big)dxds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

When
|a|\geq N_{1}-1
, we may assume that
|b+c|\leq|e|
, then we bound the left-hand side by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\phi^{(b)}\rVert_{W^{N(a),\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{W^{N(a),\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}^{4}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta-1/2+2\delta-1+\delta+H(c)\delta+H(e)\delta}ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

The case
|b+c|>|e|
can be estimated similarly. When
|a|\leq N_{1}-2
, by Hölder we can bound this contribution by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lVert\partial_{s}\phi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\big(\lVert\phi^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}+\lVert\nabla\phi^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(a)}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\big)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\epsilon_{1}\langle s\rangle^{H(a)\delta}(\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{-1/2+2\delta-1+\delta+H(c+2)\delta+H(e)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle s\rangle^{H(b)\delta-2+2\delta+H(c+2)\delta+H(e+2)\delta})ds\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{4}.

This concludes the treatment of
Step 6
. Therefore, this completes the proof of the lemma.

∎

6.
Bounds on the profile: Weighted
L^{2}
norm

In this section we prove (
2.24
), namely

Proposition 6.1
.

With the hypothesis in Proposition
2.8
, for any
t\in[0,T]
,
0\leq|a|\leq N_{1}-1
, we have

\lVert\mathcal{F}^{-1}(|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\widehat{\Psi^{(a)}})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}\langle t\rangle^{H(a+1)\delta}.

Proof.

By the bound (
2.14
) and
S=t\partial_{t}+x\cdot\partial_{x}
, we get

(6.1)

\lVert\mathcal{F}^{-1}(|\xi|\nabla_{\xi}\widehat{\Psi^{(a)}}(\xi))\rVert_{H^{N}}\lesssim\lVert S\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N}}+\lVert\Omega\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N}}+\lVert\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N}}+\lVert t\partial_{t}\Psi^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N}},

where

S\Psi^{(a)}=e^{-it|\nabla|}S\Phi^{(a)},\ \Omega\Psi^{(a)}=e^{-it|\nabla|}\Omega\Phi^{(a)},

it suffices from (
2.23
) to prove that

(6.2)

\lVert\partial_{t}\Psi^{(a)}(t)\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta}.

We only prove the case when
|t|\geq 1
, from
\phi^{(a)}
-equation in (
2.15
), it suffices to prove

(6.3)

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1},

(6.4)

\displaystyle\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\Delta\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta},

(6.5)

\displaystyle\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta},

for
b+c=a
,
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}
, and

(6.6)

\displaystyle\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla(u^{(c)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(e)})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{-1},

(6.7)

\displaystyle\lVert\frac{\Phi^{(b)}}{|\nabla|}\Phi^{(c)}\Phi^{(e)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{-1},

for
b+c+e=a
,
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}
.

Step 1: Proof of (
6.3
).

When
|a|=N_{1}-1
,
|b|\geq|c|
, by (
3.70
), (
3.29
) and (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\sum_{k}2^{N(|a|+1)k^{+}}\lVert P_{k}\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-3/2+(6+|b|)\delta}.

When
|a|=N_{1}-1
,
|b|<|c|
, using (
3.69
), it follows that

\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim(\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{N(|a|+1)k}\lVert P_{k}\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}})\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(b)/4+H(c)\delta},

When
|a|\leq N_{1}-2
, we obtain from (
3.70
), (
3.29
), (
3.2
) and (
3.69
)

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1-l(b)/4+H(c)\delta},

Hence, the bound (
6.3
) follows.

Step 2: Proof of (
6.4
).

When
|a|=|b|=N_{1}-1,c=0
, by (
3.2
) we have

\displaystyle\lVert{\varrho}^{(a)}\Delta\phi\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\lVert u^{(a)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\sum_{k}2^{N(|a|+1)k^{+}+k}\lVert P_{k}\nabla\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta}.

Then it suffices to estimate (
6.4
) when
|b|\leq N_{1}-2
.

Decomposing dyadically in frequency, we have

\displaystyle\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\Delta\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim(\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}\mathfrak{X}_{k}^{2})^{1/2},

where

\mathfrak{X}_{k}:=\lVert\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}}\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{it|\eta|}|\eta|\widehat{P_{k_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}.

We further divided
\mathfrak{X}_{k}
into high-low, low-high, and high-high case,

\mathfrak{X}_{k}\lesssim\mathfrak{X}^{hl}_{k}+\mathfrak{X}^{lh}_{k}+\mathfrak{X}^{hh}_{k},

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{hl}_{k}:=\sum_{k_{2}\leq k-5}\lVert\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{it|\eta|}|\eta|\widehat{P_{[k-3,k+3]}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{lh}_{k}:=\lVert\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{it|\eta|}|\eta|\widehat{P_{<k+6}u{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{[k-5,k+5]}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{hh}_{k}:=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2};k_{2}\geq k+5}\lVert\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{it|\eta|}|\eta|\widehat{P_{[k_{2}-3,k_{2}+3]}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}.

High-low interaction: The contribution of
\mathfrak{X}^{hl}_{k}
.

Integration by parts in
\eta
yields

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{hl}_{k}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}\sum_{k_{2}<k-5}\Big[\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\nabla_{\eta}(\eta)\widehat{P_{[k-3,k+3]}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\eta\nabla_{\eta}\widehat{P_{[k-3,k+3]}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\eta\widehat{P_{[k-3,k+3]}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)e^{it|\eta|}\nabla_{\eta}\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}\Big].

When
|a|=|b|\leq N_{1}-2,c=0
, by (
2.9
) we have

\displaystyle\big[\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}(\mathfrak{X}^{hl}_{k})^{2}\big]^{1/2}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}[\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{F}}^{-1}(|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\widehat{{\varrho}^{(b)}})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\sum_{k_{2}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert{\mathcal{F}}^{-1}(|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\widehat{\Psi^{(c)}})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}]

Since from (
6.1
), (
2.21
), (
3.68
), and (
3.29
) we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\widehat{{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi)\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}+t\lVert\partial_{t}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}+\sum_{|\tilde{b}|\leq|b|}\lVert(\nabla{\varrho}^{(\tilde{b})},\nabla u^{(\tilde{b})})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{1/2+(2+|b|)\delta}.

Hence, combining this and (
2.21
), (
2.22
) we may bound this contribution by

\displaystyle\big[\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}(\mathfrak{X}^{hl}_{k})^{2}\big]^{1/2}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(c)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1/3}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(|c|+1)\delta})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|c|+1)\delta},

When
|b|\leq N_{1}-2,|c|\geq 1
, it follows from (
2.21
), (
2.22
) and (
2.13
) that

\displaystyle\big[\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}(\mathfrak{X}^{hl}_{k})^{2}\big]^{1/2}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}[\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}+(\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}\lVert rP_{[k-3,k+3]}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}^{2})^{1/2}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert{\mathcal{F}}^{-1}(|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\Psi^{(c)})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(c)\delta}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,\alpha_{2}\leq 2}\lVert\partial_{r}\tilde{\Omega}^{\alpha_{1}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}^{1/2}\lVert\tilde{\Omega}^{\alpha_{2}}{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}^{1/2}\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{H(c)\delta}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(|c|+1)\delta})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta},

Therefore, we have

(6.8)

\big[\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}(\mathfrak{X}^{hl}_{k})^{2}\big]^{1/2}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta}.

Low-high and high-high interaction: The contribution of
\mathfrak{X}^{lh}_{k}
and
\mathfrak{X}^{hh}_{k}
.

We only estimate
\mathfrak{X}^{lh}_{k}
in detail, the term
\mathfrak{X}^{hh}_{k}
can be estimated similarly. Integration by parts in
\eta
, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}^{lh}_{k}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}\Big[\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\nabla_{\eta}(\eta)\widehat{P_{<k+6}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{[k-5,k+5]}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\eta\nabla_{\eta}\widehat{P_{<k+6}{\varrho}^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{[k-5,k+5]}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\eta\widehat{P_{<k+6}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)e^{it|\eta|}\nabla_{\eta}\widehat{P_{[k-5,k+5]}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}\Big]

Then by Young’s inequality, (
2.21
), (
2.22
) and (
2.13
), we have

(6.9)

\displaystyle\big[\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}(\mathfrak{X}^{lh}_{k})^{2}\big]^{1/2}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}[\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}+\lVert r{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)+1}}

\displaystyle+\lVert{\varrho}^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{2}}\lVert{\mathcal{F}}^{-1}(|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\widehat{\Psi^{(c)}})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(c)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(|c|+1)\delta})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta}.

From (
6.8
) and (
6.9
), the bound (
6.4
) for
|b|\leq N_{1}-2,|c|\geq 0
is obtained immediately. This completes the proof of (
6.4
).

Step 3: Proof of (
6.5
).

If
|a|=|b|=N_{1}-1,c=0
. (
3.2
) implies

(6.10)

\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\phi\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\lVert u^{(|b|)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\sum_{k}2^{N(|a|+1)k^{+}+k}\lVert\partial_{t}\phi\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta}.

Now it suffices to consider the case
|b|\leq N_{1}-2,|c|\geq 0
.

Decomposing dyadically in frequency, we have

\displaystyle\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim(\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}I_{k}^{2})^{1/2},

where

I_{k}:=\lVert\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}}\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{it|\eta|}\eta\widehat{P_{k_{1}}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}.

We further divided
I_{k}
into high-low, low-high, high-high case,

I_{k}\lesssim I^{hl}_{k}+I^{lh}_{k}+I^{hh}_{k},

where

\displaystyle I^{hl}_{k}:=\sum_{k_{2}\leq k-5}\lVert\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{it|\eta|}\eta\widehat{P_{[k-3,k+3]}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle I^{lh}_{k}:=\lVert\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{it|\eta|}\eta\widehat{P_{<k+6}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{[k-5,k+5]}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle I^{hh}_{k}:=\sum_{k_{1},k_{2};k_{2}\geq k+5}\lVert\varphi_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{it|\eta|}\eta\widehat{P_{[k_{2}-3,k_{2}+3]}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}.

High-low interaction: The contribution of
I^{hl}_{k}
.

Integration by parts in
\eta
yields

\displaystyle I^{hl}_{k}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}\sum_{k_{2}<k-5}\Big[\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\eta_{j}}{|\eta|}\eta\big)\widehat{P_{[k-3,k+3]}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{\eta_{j}}{|\eta|}\eta\partial_{\eta_{j}}\widehat{P_{[k-3,k+3]}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{\eta_{j}}{|\eta|}\eta\widehat{P_{[k-3,k+3]}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)e^{it|\eta|}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\widehat{P_{k_{2}}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}\Big].

When
|a|=|b|\leq N_{1}-2,c=0
, by (
6.1
), (
3.70
) and (
3.29
), one obtains

\displaystyle\lVert|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\widehat{u^{(b)}}(\xi)\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}+t\lVert\partial_{t}u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{1/2+(2+|b|)\delta}.

Then by (
2.9
), (
2.21
), (
2.22
), (
3.2
) we may bound this contribution by

\displaystyle\big[\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}(I^{hl}_{k})^{2}\big]^{1/2}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}[\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}+\lVert{\mathcal{F}}^{-1}(|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\widehat{u^{(b)}})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\sum_{k_{2}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert{\mathcal{F}}^{-1}(|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\widehat{\Psi^{(c)}})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(c)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1/3}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(|c|+1)\delta})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|c|+1)\delta},

When
|b|\leq N_{1}-2,|c|\geq 1
, it follows from (
2.21
), (
2.22
) and (
2.13
) that

\displaystyle\big[\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}(I^{hl}_{k})^{2}\big]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}[\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}+(\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}\lVert rP_{[k-3,k+3]}u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}^{2})^{1/2}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\Psi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(c)\delta}+\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq 1,\alpha_{2}\leq 2}\lVert\partial_{r}\tilde{\Omega}^{\alpha_{1}}v^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}^{1/2}\lVert\tilde{\Omega}^{\alpha_{2}}v^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}^{1/2}\epsilon_{1}\langle t\rangle^{H(c)\delta}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(|c|+1)\delta})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta},

Therefore, we have

(6.11)

\big[\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}(I^{hl}_{k})^{2}\big]^{1/2}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta}.

Low-high and high-high interaction: The contribution of
I^{lh}_{k}
and
I^{hh}_{k}
.

We only bound
I^{lh}_{k}
in detail, the term
I^{hh}_{k}
can be estimated similarly. Integration by parts in
\eta
, it follows that

\displaystyle I^{lh}_{k}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}\Big[\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\eta_{j}}{|\eta|}\eta\big)\widehat{P_{<k+6}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{[k-5,k+5]}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{\eta_{j}}{|\eta|}\eta\partial_{\eta_{j}}\widehat{P_{<k+6}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)\widehat{P_{[k-5,k+5]}\Phi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{\eta_{j}}{|\eta|}\eta\widehat{P_{<k+6}u^{(b)}}(\xi-\eta)e^{it|\eta|}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\widehat{P_{[k-5,k+5]}\Psi^{(c)}}(\eta)d\eta\rVert_{L^{2}}\Big]

Then by (
2.9
), (
2.21
), (
2.22
) and (
2.13
), we have

(6.12)

\displaystyle\big[\sum_{k}2^{2N(|a|+1)k^{+}}(I^{lh}_{k})^{2}\big]^{1/2}\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}[\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{2}}+\lVert ru^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\Phi^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)+1}}

\displaystyle+\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lVert{\mathcal{F}}^{-1}(|\xi|\partial_{\xi}\widehat{\Psi^{(c)}})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t^{-1}(\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(c)\delta}+\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{H(|c|+1)\delta})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}\langle t\rangle^{-1+H(|a|+1)\delta}.

From (
6.11
) and (
6.12
), the bound (
6.5
) for
|b|\leq N_{1}-2,|c|\geq 0
is obtained immediately. This completes the proof of (
6.5
).

Step 4: Proof of (
6.6
).

When
|e|\leq N_{1}-2
, using (
3.53
) and (
3.2
), we get

\displaystyle\lVert u^{(b)}\cdot\nabla(u^{(c)}\cdot\nabla\phi^{(e)})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}(\lVert\nabla u^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert u^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla^{2}\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}})

\displaystyle+\lVert u^{(b)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}(\lVert u^{(c)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)+1}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert u^{(c)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)+1}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{-5/4},

When
|e|\geq N_{1}-1
, by (
3.52
), we have

\displaystyle\lVert u\cdot\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\phi^{(e)})\rVert_{H^{N(|a|+1)}}\lesssim

\displaystyle(\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{N(|e|+1)k+k}\lVert P_{k}u\rVert_{L^{\infty}}+\lVert P_{<0}u\rVert_{L^{\infty}})^{2}\lVert\nabla\phi^{(e)}\rVert_{H^{N(|e|+1)+1}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{3}\langle t\rangle^{-5/4},

Hence, the bound (
6.6
) follows.

Finally, the bound (
6.7
) is an consequence of (
3.2
) and (
3.3
).
This completes the proof of the proposition.
∎
